# Skills · R · part 18

- **redteam**: Adversarial pass on the project's own code via the red team track - hostile inputs, error paths, security review - producing feedback docs per finding. Argument: a scope (feature, path, or task ID); defaults to the focus task's surface. ([aashishvasu/dev-team-claude](https://github.com/aashishvasu/dev-team-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/redteam))
- **redteam**: Adversarially stress-test a decision, idea, plan, or strategy using the U.S. Army UFMCS Red Team methodology — to disprove it rather than confirm it, then deliver a committed go/no-go verdict. Runs an 8-phase gauntlet (Problem Restatement, Key Assumptions + Sensitivity, Perspective Flip, Premortem, Devil's Advocacy + Alternatives, Root-Cause/Shifting-the-Burden, Leading Indicators, Verdict). Defaults to a BUSINESS STRATEGY lens (offers, channels, prospecting, pricing, GTM, growth bets) but supports security-assessment and content domains via a flag. Use when the user says "red team this", "re… ([anthonyonazure/content-hub](https://github.com/anthonyonazure/content-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/redteam))
- **redteam**: Revisión multidimensional adversarial. Solo en tareas Must-have. La seguridad es un gate obligatorio, no una dimensión ponderada más. ([apuherrerafo/D2KIRO](https://github.com/apuherrerafo/D2KIRO/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/redteam))
- **redteam**: Security red team assessment — recon + parallel specialist agents + lead analyst synthesis ([djwmobley/pipeline](https://github.com/djwmobley/pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/redteam))
- **redteam**: Executa uma rodada de testes adversariais defensivos contra o projeto atual (esta aplicacao, seus endpoints, sua autenticacao e qualquer agente/LLM embutido). Use antes de deploy, ao fechar uma fase com item de risco medio/alto no PLANO_MESTRE.md, ou quando o Operador pedir uma auditoria de seguranca. Nunca mira sistemas de terceiros. ([ENDARTStudios/Almanaque-dos-Clubes](https://github.com/ENDARTStudios/Almanaque-dos-Clubes/tree/HEAD/docs))
- **RedTeam**: Adversarial analysis with 32 agents. USE WHEN red team, attack idea, counterarguments, critique, stress test. SkillSearch('redteam') for docs. ([fadrienne/Apps](https://github.com/fadrienne/Apps/tree/HEAD/Personal_AI_Infrastructure/Releases/v2.4/.claude/skills/RedTeam) · 4 copies)
- **RedTeam**: Military-grade adversarial analysis deploying 32 parallel expert agents (engineers, architects, pentesters, interns) to stress-test ideas, strategies, and plans — not systems. Two workflows: ParallelAnalysis (decompose into 24 atomic claims → 32-agent parallel attack → synthesis → steelman → counter-argument) and AdversarialValidation (competing proposals synthesized into best solution). Targets arguments, not network vulnerabilities. Severity-ranked findings with remediation. Invoked by Ideate (TEST) and WorldThreatModel. USE WHEN red team, attack idea, counterarguments, critique, stress tes… ([fadrienne/Apps](https://github.com/fadrienne/Apps/tree/HEAD/Personal_AI_Infrastructure/LifeOS/install/skills/RedTeam))
- **redteam**: Spawn a one-shot red-team subagent to challenge a milestone result. Calibrated to return "nothing substantive" when the work is sound — does not invent issues to seem thorough. Use at goal-locked, major-result, or pre-write-up milestones, or whenever you want a deliberate adversarial check on something specific. ([frodo2647/triptych](https://github.com/frodo2647/triptych/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/redteam))
- **redteam**: Pressure-tests a plan, idea, draft, or decision by switching into genuine-critic mode on demand. Use when the user says /redteam, "poke holes in this," "red team this," "what's wrong with this plan," "argue against this," "stress-test this," "where does this break," or "be my critic." Puts the strongest honest objections on the table: failure modes, hidden assumptions, weakest links, what a smart skeptic would say, and what would have to be true for this to fail. Deliberately adversarial and specific — no reflexive agreement, no vague caveats. Critique only; it does not rewrite or fix unless… ([jbookout/carr-system](https://github.com/jbookout/carr-system/tree/HEAD/corpus/_home/.claude/skills/redteam))
- **redteam**: Attack a plan or a claim on its own terms. Steelman the strongest objection, name the assumption the whole thing rests on, and report only what would actually change the decision. - **RedTeam**: Adversarial review that sends parallel expert critics after an idea, plan, or strategy — splitting it into small claims, attacking each, then returning a ranked severity report with fixes.
